PORT CITY

A free neon open-world city game. Take jobs, steal the show, outrun the cops and make the city yours.

▶ Play in browser⬇ Android app (APK)
Free · No account · Works on phone and computer

One city. Your rules.

Drive through a glowing 3D city, find the people marked with a ! and work your way up.

Missions

Meet colorful characters around the city and take jobs for cash.

Cars & garage

Steal or buy a ride, then customize it in the garage.

Wanted level

Cause trouble and the stars go up. The cops will come for you.

Make it yours

Visit the wardrobe to change skin, outfit, hair, hats and shades.

Maps

A soft minimap in the corner and a full-screen city map with every shop.

Save anywhere

Autosave and manual save keep your cash, guns and progress.

See it in action

Real screenshots from the game.

Controls

The goal: find the colored people with a ! to take jobs. The gun shop sells weapons and the hospital heals.

Computer

Arrows / WASDMove or drive
ShiftSprint (horn in car)
SpaceJump (handbrake in car)
Click / CtrlFire
Enter or FEnter car, talk, shop
MBig map
KAvatar
O or F5Save
PPause

Phone

Left thumbMove stick
FIREShoots with auto-aim
Round buttonEnter, exit, talk, shop
GUNSwitch weapon
BRKBrake and reverse
Tap minimapOpen big map

Tip: rotate your phone to landscape.

Get the game

Play in your browser

Nothing to install. Works on most phones and computers.

▶ Play now

Install on Android

  1. Tap the download button below.
  2. Open the downloaded PortCity.apk.
  3. If asked, allow Install unknown apps for your browser.
⬇ Download APK

FAQ

Is it free?

Yes. The Android app may show short ads and has an optional button to watch an ad for bonus cash.

Is the APK safe?

It is built automatically from the game's public code on GitHub. Android shows an "unknown source" warning for any app that doesn't come from the Play Store.

Does it work offline?

The Android app works offline. In the browser, it needs a connection the first time you load it.

Where is my save stored?

On your own device, in the app or browser. Clearing the browser's site data deletes a browser save.

iPhone?

Open the browser version in Safari, then Share and Add to Home Screen.
